This site uses cookies to ensure you get the best experience on our website.</p></p><div class="ccc-expanded"><p>Some cookies on this site are essential, and the site won't work as expected without them. These cookies are set when you submit a form, login or interact with the site by doing something that goes beyond clicking on simple links.</p><p>We also use some non-essential cookies to anonymously track visitors or enhance your experience of the site. If you're not happy with this, we won't set these cookies but some nice features of the site may be unavailable.</p><p>By using our site you accept the terms of our <a href="https://www.jamesbondlifestyle.com/user-consent-policy">Privacy Policy</a>.</p></div><div id="ccc-cookies-switch" style="background-position-x: 0;"><a href="#" id="cctoggle" name="cctoggle" style="background-position-x: 0;"><span class="cctoggle-text-on" id="cctoggle-text">Cookies are on</span></a></div><div id="ccc-implicit-warning">(One cookie will be set to store your preference)</div><div id="ccc-explicit-checkbox"><label><input id="cchide-popup" name="ccc-hide-popup" type="checkbox" value="Y"/> Do not ask me again<br/></label> (Ticking this sets a cookie to hide this popup if you then hit close. <h2 class="pane-title">
      "Welcome, Mr. Bond!"    </h2>
<div class="pane-content">
<h2 class="pane-title">About Bond Lifestyle</h2><p style="margin-top: 10px;">I would like to welcome you to Bond Lifestyle, the most stylish and clear online guide to the gadgets, the clothes, the cars, the travel locations and the gambling habits of the most suave secret agent. Find inspiration to improve your style, complete your 007 collection, get ideas for your James Bond theme party, or identify that shirt that you saw in the latest Bond movie <strong>SPECTRE</strong>. Read more <a href="node/373">about Bond Lifestyle here.</a></p><p style="margin-top: 10px;">If you have any questions, suggestions... or compliments, contact me <a href="http://www.jamesbondlifestyle.com/contact">here</a>.</p><p style="margin-top: 10px;">Remmert van Braam</p><p><strong>"... it's time to revisit essential flourishes of Bond style: www.jamesbondlifestyle.com is akin to an online butler guiding you through the wardrobe ... and travel destinations..." </strong><br/><em>-The Times Online</em></p> </div>
